SAN FRANCISCO / RESEARCH INDEXSnapshot: 18 October 2026 · Directory CSV ↓

CITY ONLY. SOURCES FIRST.

A trail back to the source.

Explicit field decisions and quotations, not inherited verification badges. Every candidate keeps official links for manual review.

How evidence becomes a published field

  1. Historical inputs are consolidated into candidates and saved as unverified history, not trusted facts.
  2. Official organizer, venue, museum, library and government pages are read in context. Public-tourism sources are discovery/reference material, not a substitute for missing event details.
  3. Each accepted name, schedule, time, venue, admission condition, recurrence and occurrence date has an explicit decision and source quotation. Structured event JSON-LD is labeled separately from readable page text. Selected tool excerpts are not represented as complete raw page captures.
  4. The newer companion research audit and its two added candidates are preserved as unverified history before the final gate. Its old field/page labels do not automatically approve values.
  5. The build starts every factual field blank and copies only approved decisions. Name, venue and documented SF geography are required for directory admission. Other unknowns remain blank and flagged. Geography can identify a recognized SF venue from its official address/directions; it is never inferred from a stray city token in unrelated marketing.
  6. Exact-quote checks test provenance, not semantic truth. Context, edition, eligibility and conflicting copy require a research judgment. A URL/status/name match never verifies the rest of a row.
  7. Only explicitly approved ISO occurrences determine labels relative to the fixed snapshot. URL slugs, weekdays, copyright dates and neighboring events never supply missing years. Recurrence evidence does not generate future dates.
  8. Cost labels are computed only from the verified admission text. “Free”, “Paid” and “Not stated” are labels over that quote; the exact official wording is always shown beside the badge. A blank admission cell is never published as free.
  9. Topic tags (Gaming / Music / AI & technology) are editorial navigation labels derived from the verified event name, or from an explicit decision for the new-requirement research pass. They are not a verified field.

Times are local to San Francisco. Free parking, free activities with paid entry, and virtual tickets do not establish free physical admission. Conditions such as residency, age, surcharges and RSVP remain visible.

Evidence snapshots: archived captures and explicit decisions (JSON) · gaming, music and AI research pass (JSON). Some captures in the newer pass are the verbatim text the workspace search tool returned for an exact official URL rather than a full page fetch (sfpl.org branch navigation, the Stern Grove contact block); those records carry no HTTP status and say so. Original HTTP status and later attempt metadata remain separate; failed refreshes do not erase prior readable evidence. This is not continuous re-verification.

All audited records · Source ledger · Directory CSV · Review CSV · UNVERIFIED inherited history

Row-by-row field evidence

Only the values shown below are approved facts. A blank field remains unverified, even when the page or event name is confirmed. Read the full linked page to check current context.
